ParlaMint-PL_2017-06-21-sejm-44-2.u366	Dear Marshal, Mr. Messengers! Gentlemen! On behalf of the Poselski Club Modern I have the honour to present a position on the parliamentary draft law amending the Personal Income Tax Act, print no. 1613. With the requested submission, Members seek to remove the limit of PLN 42 764 for the application of 50% flat-rate costs of earning revenue by the creators. There is no doubt that the determination of the cost of obtaining revenue in the case of the creative process is significantly impeded. It is not possible to objective and value the expenditures of work - years of acquiring knowledge and relevant skills - in relation to a particular product of artistic or creative activity. This is why this problem has already been recognised in the pre-war period and it has been introduced for the creators that the cost of obtaining revenue in the form of a lump sum. Unfortunately, the legislator introduced a 50% limitation in 2012 flat-rate revenue costs by setting an annual limit of PLN 42 764 per year. The proposed amendment restores legal status before 2013. Unfortunately, the proposed amendments also contain an important drawback of not taking into account the reduction in the income of local government units. It should be noted that a significant part of the income tax on individuals constitutes respectively the income of municipalities, powiats and voivodships. The increase in income tax revenues from individuals has been introduced into local government units as newer and newer own tasks, the source of which would be this tax. Therefore, particular consideration should be given to the proposals to reduce income tax revenues, and this is undoubtedly the result of the proposed changes, without adequate financial compensation for local government units. However, the proposed bill does not provide for such solutions. However, I hope that the appropriate changes will be proposed in committee work. In view of the above, the Posel Club The modern recommendation is to refer the bill to further work in the committee. Thank you.

The University of Opole was the first university in Poland after the Second World War, which in its structure formed the theological department, apart from KUL of course. His involvement in humanitarian aid during and after the martial law is known throughout Poland. Firefighters with TSOs have fire equipment, but it would be worthless if they didn't train the next generation of firefighters. Firefighters, taking a number of actions, e.g. organizing the Firefighter's Sports Festival for Children, shows or watching the matches of the Polish team together, make their unit the center of Brzezinka's cultural life. Special thanks are due to President Tomasz Włoczyk, who has actively contributed to the establishment and development of the CSR unit in Brzezinka. The Firefighter's Day celebrations, which took place on 7 May, accompanied the Holy Mass in the parish of St. Jadwiga and the dedication of a new combat car, in which I helped to deliver to the Volunteer Fire Service in Birkenau. I wish to wish all firefighters once again, especially those from Gliwice-Birkenau, to leave for action as rarely as possible, but to return home safe and sound. There is a reason why firefighters enjoy the greatest social respect they deserve. You can always count on them, they're always there when you need them, no matter what the circumstances. Often by risking their lives, they help us, save our lives, or save our entire accomplishments. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-06-21-sejm-44-2.u386	Mr. Marshal, thank you very much. Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Let me say a few sentences about the Bands and Singing Teams Festival in Stara Błotnica near Radom. The commune's wójt Stara Błotnica Mr.Marcin Kozdach once again organized a fantastic festival with his coworkers and residents, thanks to which often forgotten folk songs and songs resonated on Radom soil. This event always has great interest in both performers and invited guests. Beautiful music and colorful regional outfits enjoy the senses and bring back memories of old times. This year more than 20 bands and singing bands from all over Radom's land took part in the festival, among others. Teams from Radom, Lesiów, Ostrołęki, Marianowic, Domaniowa, Guzowa, Czarolasu, Dzielna, Opoczna, Zwolenia, Wawrzyszów, Mysiedla, Mirowa, Marianów and of course from Stara Błotnica. The bands presented folk music and costumes characteristic for their region. Performers are mostly older people who have been pursuing their passions for many years and make sure that both music and folk songs are passed on to young generations. The Festival of Singing bands and bands was preceded by Holy Mass. at the Sanctuary of Our Lady of Comfort. Then the participants marched on the square with a colorful corrode, next to the municipal office, where the performances began. It is very important to maintain the tradition of the region through music. This is a huge part of our culture. Each region has its own distinctive songs, which, of course, despite similarities are unique and unique. With folk music, not only the body, but also the soul struggles to dance, because each of us has a longing for simplicity, beauty, which brings a simple life in the country and a beautiful rural culture. I am very pleased that in my region, thanks to the involvement of local authorities, thanks to the involvement of your uncle, such events can take place on a regular basis and are of great interest. From this point of view, I would like to thank you very much, both to your uncle and to the entire municipality, and to all those who have committed themselves as every year to organizing this excellent festival. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
